Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on riemannian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 602. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 247 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the riemannian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^602 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 247-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
